a_{0},a_{k}\geq\frac{n-1}{2}
, then

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{kn}}\frac{\langle x_{1}\rangle^{-\beta_{1}-}\cdots\langle x_{k}\rangle^{-\beta_{k}-}{\rm d}x_{1}\cdots{\rm d}x_{k}}{\langle x_{i_{0}}-x_{i_{0}+1}\rangle^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}\setminus\{i_{0}\}}|x_{i}-x_{i+1}|^{a_{i}}\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}}|E_{\eta_{i},i}|^{n+1-4m}}\lesssim 1,\quad 0<|x_{0}-x_{k+1}|\lesssim 1.

Proof.

The same type of argument from (
5.75
) to (
5.83
) with the help of the second assertion of Proposition
5.15
shall finally reduce the estimate to the case of
k=2
. Without repeating the discussion, we only prove when
k=2
in the following.

To show 1), note that
\eta_{2}=0
or
\eta_{2}=1
holds, and

\begin{split}&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\frac{\langle x_{2}\rangle^{-\beta_{2}-}{\rm d}x_{2}}{|x_{1}-x_{2}|^{a_{1}}|x_{2}-x_{3}|^{a_{2}}|E_{x_{0}x_{1}x_{1}x_{2}}|^{n+1-4m}|E_{x_{\eta_{2}}x_{\eta_{2}+1}x_{2}x_{3}}|^{n+1-4m}}\\
\lesssim&|E_{x_{0}x_{1}x_{1}x_{3}}|^{-(n+1-4m)}|x_{1}-x_{3}|^{-\min\{a_{1},a_{2}\}},\quad|x_{1}-x_{3}|>0,\end{split}

where in the case of
\eta_{2}=0
we have used

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{|E_{x_{0}x_{1}x_{1}x_{2}}|^{n+1-4m}|E_{x_{0}x_{1}x_{2}x_{3}}|^{n+1-4m}}\\
\lesssim&\frac{1}{|E_{x_{0}x_{1}x_{1}x_{2}}|^{n+1-4m}|E_{x_{1}x_{2}x_{2}x_{3}}|^{n+1-4m}}+\frac{1}{|E_{x_{0}x_{1}x_{2}x_{3}}|^{n+1-4m}|E_{x_{1}x_{2}x_{2}x_{3}}|^{n+1-4m}}.\end{split}

Therefore Proposition
2.4
implies

I_{2}\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\frac{\langle x_{1}\rangle^{-\beta_{1}-}{\rm d}x_{1}}{|x_{0}-x_{1}|^{a_{0}}|x_{1}-x_{3}|^{\min\{a_{1},a_{2}\}}|E_{x_{0}x_{1}x_{1}x_{3}}|^{n+1-4m}}\lesssim 1,\quad 0<|x_{0}-x_{3}|\lesssim 1.

We next show 2). If
k=2
, then
i_{0}=1
, and one checks with Proposition
2.4
using
a_{0}\geq\frac{n-1}{2}
that

\begin{split}&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\frac{\langle x_{1}\rangle^{-\beta_{1}-}\mathrm{d}x_{1}}{|x_{0}-x_{1}|^{a_{0}}\langle x_{1}-x_{2}\rangle^{\frac{n-1}{2}}|E_{x_{0}x_{1}x_{1}x_{2}}|^{n+1-4m}|E_{x_{\eta_{2}}x_{\eta_{2}+1}x_{2}x_{3}}|^{n+1-4m}}\\
\lesssim&|E_{x_{0}x_{2}x_{2}x_{3}}|^{-(n+1-4m)},\quad|x_{0}-x_{2}|>0,\end{split}

and therefore

\begin{split}I_{2}\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\frac{\langle x_{2}\rangle^{-\beta_{2}-}\mathrm{d}x_{2}}{|x_{2}-x_{3}|^{a_{3}}|E_{x_{0}x_{2}x_{2}x_{3}}|^{n+1-4m}}\lesssim 1,\quad 0<|x_{0}-x_{3}|\lesssim 1.\end{split}

∎

The following is our second main technical result in Section
5
.

Proposition 5.19
.

We have

I^{(1)}:=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{kn}}\frac{X^{\frac{n-1}{2}}|V^{(\alpha_{1})}(x_{1})|\cdots|V^{(\alpha_{k})}(x_{k})|{\rm d}x_{1}\cdots{\rm d}x_{k}}{|r_{0}|^{n-2-l_{0}+d_{0}}\cdots|r_{k}|^{n-2-l_{k}+d_{k}}\|F_{1}\|^{p_{1}}\cdots\|F_{s}\|^{p_{s}}}\lesssim 1,\quad|x_{0}-x_{k+1}|\gtrsim 1,

and

I^{(2)}:=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{kn}}\frac{X^{n-L_{k}-2m}|V^{(\alpha_{1})}(x_{1})|\cdots|V^{(\alpha_{k})}(x_{k})|{\rm d}x_{1}\cdots{\rm d}x_{k}}{|r_{0}|^{n-2-l_{0}+d_{0}}\cdots|r_{k}|^{n-2-l_{k}+d_{k}}\|F_{1}\|^{p_{1}}\cdots\|F_{s}\|^{p_{s}}}\lesssim 1,\quad|x_{0}-x_{k+1}|\lesssim 1,

where
V
,
F_{i}
and all the indices are the same ones in (
5.12
).

Proof.

To estimate
I^{(1)}
, Proposition
5.14
first implies either

\begin{split}I^{(1)}\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{kn}}&X^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}}|V^{(\alpha_{i})}(x_{i})|\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}}|r_{i}|^{-(n-2-l_{i}+d_{i})}\\
&\quad\quad\times|E_{\iota_{j^{(i_{0})}},j^{(i_{0})}}|^{-(\frac{n+1}{2}-2m)}\prod_{j\in\mathbb{K}\setminus\{j^{(i_{0})}\}}|E_{\iota_{j},j}|^{-(n+1-4m)}{\rm d}x_{1}\cdots{\rm d}x_{k},\end{split}

(5.89)

or

\begin{split}I^{(1)}\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{kn}}&X^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}}|V^{(\alpha_{i})}(x_{i})|\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}}|r_{i}|^{-(n-2-l_{i}+d_{i})}\\
&\times|E_{\iota_{k},k}|^{-\min\{n+1-4m,\frac{n+1}{2}-2m+d_{0}+d_{k}\}}\prod_{j\in\mathbb{K}\setminus\{k\}}|E_{\iota_{j},j}|^{-(n+1-4m)}{\rm d}x_{1}\cdots{\rm d}x_{k},\end{split}

(5.90)

where in the first estimate,
j^{(i_{0})}\leq\iota_{j}
holds for all
j\in\{j^{(i_{0})}+1,\cdots,k\}
if
j^{(i_{0})}<k
. Recall that
\{(\iota_{j},j;);j\in\mathbb{K}\}
is admissible by Proposition
5.11
.

We now decompose the RHS of either of these estimates into
I^{(1)}_{j}
(
j\in\mathbb{K}_{0}
) according to the regions
D_{j}=\{X\sim|r_{j}|\}
.

If
j\notin\{0,k\}
, then in
D_{j}

\begin{split}&X^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}}|V^{(\alpha_{i})}(x_{i})|\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}}|r_{i}|^{-(n-2-l_{i}+d_{i})}\\
\lesssim&\langle x_{1}\rangle^{-\frac{n+1}{2}-}\langle x_{k}\rangle^{-\frac{n+1}{2}-}\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}\setminus\{1,k\}}\langle x_{i}\rangle^{-2m-}|r_{0}|^{-(n-2-l_{0}+d_{0})}|r_{k}|^{-(n-2-l_{k}+d_{k})}\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}}|r_{i}|^{-\max\{n-2-l_{i}+d_{i},n-2m\}},\end{split}

where we have used the decay
|V^{(\alpha_{i})}(x_{i})|\lesssim\langle x_{i}\rangle^{-\frac{3n+1}{2}-2m-}
,

\langle x_{i}\rangle^{-1}\langle x_{i+1}\rangle^{-1}\lesssim\langle x_{1}-x_{i+1}\rangle^{-1}\leq|x_{1}-x_{i+1}|^{-1},

(5.91)

n-2-l_{i}+d_{i}\geq\frac{n-1}{2}
and
n-2m-\min\{n-2-l_{i}+d_{i},n-2m\}\leq\frac{n+1}{2}-2m
. Now the RHS of both (
5.89
) and (
5.90
) when restricted in
D_{j}
can be estimated as

\begin{split}I^{(1)}_{j}\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{kn}}&\langle x_{1}\rangle^{-\frac{n+1}{2}-}\langle x_{k}\rangle^{-\frac{n+1}{2}-}\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}\setminus\{1,k\}}\langle x_{i}\rangle^{-2m-}|r_{0}|^{-(n-2-l_{0}+d_{0})}\\
&\times|r_{k}|^{-(n-2-l_{k}+d_{k})}\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}\setminus\{0,k\}}|r_{i}|^{-\max\{n-2-l_{i}+d_{i},n-2m\}}\prod_{j\in\mathbb{K}}|E_{\iota_{j},j}|^{-(n+1-4m)}{\rm d}x_{1}\cdots{\rm d}x_{k},\end{split}

and the desired bound is checked by Lemma
5.16
.

Similarly in
D_{0}
or
D_{k}
, it follows that

\begin{split}X^{\frac{n-1}{2}}\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}}|V^{(\alpha_{i})}(x_{i})|\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}}|r_{i}|^{-(n-2-l_{i}+d_{i})}\lesssim\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}}\langle x_{i}\rangle^{-\frac{n+1}{2}-}|r_{0}|^{-a_{0}}|r_{k}|^{-a_{k}}\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}}|r_{i}|^{-\max\{n-2-l_{i}+d_{i},n-2m\}},\end{split}

where either

a_{0}+a_{k}\geq n-2-l_{0}-d_{0}+d_{k}\geq\mbox{$\frac{n-1}{2}$}+d_{0}+d_{k},

or

a_{0}+a_{k}\geq n-2-l_{k}+d_{0}+d_{k}\geq\mbox{$\frac{n-1}{2}$}+d_{0}+d_{k},

and we have used
n-2-l_{i}-\frac{n-1}{2}\geq 0
. So for
I^{(1)}_{k}
, we have

\begin{split}I^{(1)}_{k}\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{kn}}&\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}}\langle x_{i}\rangle^{-\frac{n+1}{2}-}|r_{0}|^{-a_{0}}|r_{k}|^{-a_{k}}\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}\setminus\{0,k\}}|r_{i}|^{-\max\{n-2-l_{i}+d_{i},n-2m\}}\\
&\times|E_{\iota_{j^{(i_{0})}},j^{(i_{0})}}|^{-(\frac{n+1}{2}-2m)}\prod_{j\in\mathbb{K}\setminus\{j^{(i_{0})}\}}|E_{\iota_{j},j}|^{-(n+1-4m)}{\rm d}x_{1}\cdots{\rm d}x_{k},\end{split}

or

\begin{split}I^{(1)}_{k}\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{kn}}&\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}}\langle x_{i}\rangle^{-\frac{n+1}{2}-}|r_{0}|^{-a_{0}}|r_{k}|^{-a_{k}}\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}\setminus\{0,k\}}|r_{i}|^{-\max\{n-2-l_{i}+d_{i},n-2m\}}\\
&\times|E_{\iota_{k},k}|^{-\min\{n+1-4m,\frac{n+1}{2}-2m+d_{0}+d_{k}\}}\prod_{j\in\mathbb{K}\setminus\{k\}}|E_{\iota_{j},j}|^{-(n+1-4m)}{\rm d}x_{1}\cdots{\rm d}x_{k},\end{split}

and the desired bound for
I^{(1)}_{k}
can be checked by Lemma
5.16
and Lemma
5.17
, while the bound for
I^{(1)}_{0}
follows in the same way.

To estimate
I^{(2)}
, let
L_{k}=l_{i_{1}}+2-2m
for some
i_{1}\in\mathbb{K}_{0}
. First note that in
D_{j}
we have

X^{n-L_{k}-2m}|r_{j}|^{-(n-2-l_{j}+d_{j})}\lesssim|r_{j}|^{-(l_{i_{1}}-l_{j}+d_{j})}.

If
l_{j}+2-2m\leq 0
, then
j\notin I_{0,2}^{*}
,
d_{j}=0
and

\begin{split}\sum_{i\in I_{0,2}^{*}}(n-2-l_{i}+d_{i})+l_{i_{1}}-l_{j}\leq&\sum_{i\in I_{0,2}^{*}\setminus\{i_{1}\}}(n-2-l_{i})+\sum_{i=k_{0}}^{k-1}L_{i}+(n-2-l_{j})\\
\leq&(n-2m)(k-k_{0})+n-2-l_{j}\leq(n-2)(k-k_{0}+1).\end{split}

Since
\#I_{0,2}^{*}=k-k_{0}+1
and
\frac{n-1}{2}\leq n-2-l_{i}+d_{i}\leq n-2m
holds for
i\in I_{0,2}^{*}
, we have the existence of
\delta_{i}\geq 0
for
i\in I_{0,2}^{*}
such that
\frac{n-1}{2}\leq n-2-l_{i}+d_{i}+\delta_{i}\leq n-2
holds, and

\sum_{i\in I_{0,2}^{*}}(n-2-l_{i}+d_{i}+\delta_{i})=\sum_{i\in I_{0,2}^{*}}(n-2-l_{i}+d_{i})+l_{i_{1}}-l_{j}.

This, together with (
5.91
) and the fact that
|r_{i}|\lesssim|r_{j}|
for
i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}
, implies the following bounds. We decompose
I^{(2)}
into
I^{(2)}_{j}
(
j=0,\cdots,k
) by restricting the integral in
D_{j}
. If
j\notin\{0,k\}
, then
I^{(2)}_{j}
is bounded by

\begin{split}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{kn}}&\langle x_{j-1}-x_{j}\rangle^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}}\langle x_{i}\rangle^{-2m-}\prod_{i\in I_{0,2}^{*}}|r_{i}|^{-\max\{n-2-l_{i}+d_{i}+\delta_{i},n-2m\}}\prod_{i\in\{0,k\}}|r_{i}|^{-(n-2-l_{i}+d_{i})}\\
&\times\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}\setminus(I_{0,2}^{*}\cup\{j\}\cup\{0,k\})}|r_{i}|^{-\max\{n-2-l_{i}+d_{i},n-2m\}}\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}}|E_{\iota_{i},i}|^{-(n+1-4m)}{\rm d}x_{1}\cdots{\rm d}x_{k}.\end{split}

If
j\in\{0,k\}
, then
I^{(2)}_{j}
is bounded by

\begin{split}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{kn}}&\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}}\langle x_{i}\rangle^{-2m-}\prod_{i\in I_{0,2}^{*}}|r_{i}|^{-\max\{n-2-l_{i}+d_{i}+\delta_{i},n-2m\}}\prod_{i\in\{0,k\}\setminus\{j\}}|r_{i}|^{-(n-2-l_{i}+d_{i})}\\
&\times\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}\setminus(I_{0,2}^{*}\cup\{j\}\cup\{0,k\})}|r_{i}|^{-\max\{n-2-l_{i}+d_{i},n-2m\}}\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}}|E_{\iota_{i},i}|^{-(n+1-4m)}{\rm d}x_{1}\cdots{\rm d}x_{k}.\end{split}

These bounds can be estimated by Lemma
5.18
, and we remark that the conditions for
a_{0}
and
a_{k}
in the two cases of Lemma
5.18
follow from the fact that
n-2-l_{i}+d_{i}\geq\frac{n-1}{2}
when
i=0,k
, and the other conditions are easy to check.

If
l_{j}+2-2m>0
, then
j\in I_{0,2}^{*}
and

\sum_{i\in I_{0,2}^{*}\setminus\{j\}}(n-2-l_{i}+d_{i})+l_{i_{1}}-l_{j}+d_{j}\leq(n-2m)(k-k_{0}),

so the estimate can also be similarly reduced to the application of Lemma
5.18
, and we save the parallel details here.
∎

5.5.
Completing the estimate for
\Omega_{k}^{high,2}(t,x,y)

Recall that we are now left to obtain estimate (
4.11
) for
I^{\vec{l}}(t;x,y)
defined by (
4.10
) under the assumption (
4.16
), which completes the estimate (
4.5
) for
\Omega_{k}^{high,2}(t,x,y)
.

Apply Proposition
5.1
with
\mu=k-k_{0}
(and
T
replaced by
\delta T
there) to the spatial integrals in (
4.10
), and note that
J=L_{k_{0}}+\cdots+K_{k-1}
must hold by (
5.14
), we have

\begin{split}&I^{\vec{l}}(t;x,y)\\
=&\int_{0}^{+\infty}e^{\mathrm{i}t\lambda^{2m}}\tilde{\chi}(\lambda^{2m})\lambda^{2m-1-\sum_{i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}}(2m-2-l_{i})-L_{k_{0}}-\cdots-L_{k-1}}\\
&\times\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{kn}}e^{\pm\mathrm{i}\lambda X}\frac{\left(\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}}V^{(\alpha_{i})}(x_{i})\right)g(\lambda,r_{0},\cdots,r_{k},F_{1},\cdots,F_{s})\psi(\mbox{$\frac{X}{\delta T}$})}{\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}}|r_{i}|^{n-2-l_{i}+d_{i}}\prod_{i=1}^{s}\|F_{i}\|^{p_{i}}}{\rm d}x_{1}\cdots{\rm d}x_{k}\right){\rm d}\lambda\\
=&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{kn}}\frac{\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}}V^{(\alpha_{i})}(x_{i})}{\prod_{i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}}|r_{i}|^{n-2-l_{i}+d_{i}}\prod_{i=1}^{s}\|F_{i}\|^{p_{i}}}\psi(\mbox{$\frac{X}{\delta T}$}){\rm d}x_{1}\cdots{\rm d}x_{k}\\
&\times\int_{0}^{+\infty}e^{\mathrm{i}t\lambda^{2m}\pm\mathrm{i}\lambda X}\tilde{\chi}(\lambda^{2m})\lambda^{2m-1-\sum_{i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}}(2m-2-l_{i})-L_{k_{0}}-\cdots-L_{k-1}}g(\lambda,r_{0},\cdots,r_{k},F_{1},\cdots,F_{s}){\rm d}\lambda,\end{split}

(5.92)

with all properties illustrated in Proposition
5.1
, where we note that
X\geq\frac{1}{2}\delta T\sim T
holds in
\mathrm{supp}\,\psi(\frac{X}{\delta T})
. By definition of
L_{i}
in (
5.6
), we have

\sum_{i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}}(2m-2-l_{i})+L_{k_{0}}+\cdots+L_{k-1}\geq-L_{k}=2m-2-l_{i_{0}},

where
i_{0}=\sigma(k)
(see (
5.6
)), and consequently

\tilde{\chi}(\lambda^{2m})\lambda^{2m-1-\sum_{i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}}(2m-2-l_{i})-L_{k_{0}}-\cdots-L_{k-1}}g(\lambda,r_{0},\cdots,r_{k},F_{1},\cdots,F_{s})\in S^{1+l_{i_{0}}}\left(\mbox{$(\frac{\lambda_{0}}{2},+\infty)$}\right).

Thus Lemma
2.6
gives

\begin{split}&\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}e^{\mathrm{i}t\lambda^{2m}\pm\mathrm{i}\lambda X}\tilde{\chi}(\lambda^{2m})\lambda^{2m-1-\sum_{i\in\mathbb{K}_{0}}(2m-2-l_{i})-L_{k_{0}}-\cdots-L_{k-1}}g(\lambda,r_{0},\cdots,r_{k},F_{1},\cdots,F_{s}){\rm d}\lambda\right|\\
\lesssim&|t|^{-\frac{1}{2}+\mu_{1+l_{i_{0}}}}X^{-\mu_{1+l_{i_{0}}}},\quad\mbox{$X\geq\frac{\delta T}{2}\gtrsim T$},\end{split}

and we recall that
\mu_{1+l_{i_{0}}}
is defined by (
2.6
). Similar to the calculation in (
4.14
) and (
4.15
) (recall
l_{i_{0}}=\max_{j\in\mathbb{K}_{0}}l_{j}
by definition of
L_{k}
in (
5.6
)), it follows when
X\gtrsim T
that

|t|^{-\frac{1}{2}+\mu_{1+l_{i_{0}}}}X^{-\mu_{1+l_{i_{0}}}-\frac{n-1}{2}}\text{~and~}|t|^{-\frac{1}{2}+\mu_{1+l_{i_{0}}}}X^{-\mu_{1+l_{i_{0}}}-(n-L_{k}-2m)},

are both bounded by the function

\begin{cases}|t|^{-\frac{n}{2}}(1+|t|^{-1}|x-y|)^{-\frac{n(m-1)}{2m-1}},\quad&|t|\gtrsim 1,\\
|t|^{-\frac{n}{2m}}(1+|t|^{-\frac{1}{2m}}|x-y|)^{-\frac{n(m-1)}{2m-1}},&0<|t|\lesssim 1.\end{cases}

(5.93)

If
|x-y|\gtrsim 1
, we conclude

|I^{\vec{l}}(t,x,y)|\lesssim\eqref{est}\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{kn}}\frac{X^{\frac{n-1}{2}}|V^{(\alpha_{1})}(x_{1})|\cdots|V^{(\alpha_{k})}(x_{k})|{\rm d}x_{1}\cdots{\rm d}x_{k}}{|r_{0}|^{n-2-l_{0}+d_{0}}\cdots|r_{k}|^{n-2-l_{k}+d_{k}}\|F_{1}\|^{p_{1}}\cdots\|F_{s}\|^{p_{s}}},

and if
|x-y|\lesssim 1
, we conclude

|I^{\vec{l}}(t,x,y)|\lesssim\eqref{est}\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{kn}}\frac{X^{n-L_{k}-2m}|V^{(\alpha_{1})}(x_{1})|\cdots|V^{(\alpha_{k})}(x_{k})|{\rm d}x_{1}\cdots{\rm d}x_{k}}{|r_{0}|^{n-2-l_{0}+d_{0}}\cdots|r_{k}|^{n-2-l_{k}+d_{k}}\|F_{1}\|^{p_{1}}\cdots\|F_{s}\|^{p_{s}}},

so the proof is completed by Proposition
5.19
.

Appendix   A
The proof of Proposition
2.4

Recall the quantities
E_{xyyz}
and
E_{ww^{\prime}xy}
defined in (
2.5
), and we introduce two coordinates for
y\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
:

y=\begin{cases}\mbox{$\frac{x+z}{2}+s_{y}\frac{(x-z)}{|x-z|}+h_{y}$},\quad&s_{y}\in\mathbb{R},\,h_{y}\in(x-z)^{\perp}\cong\mathbb{R}^{n-1},\\
\mbox{$x+\tilde{s}_{y}\frac{(w^{\prime}-w)}{|w^{\prime}-w|}+\tilde{h}_{y}$},&\tilde{s}_{y}\in\mathbb{R},\,\tilde{h}_{y}\in(w^{\prime}-w)^{\perp}\cong\mathbb{R}^{n-1}.\end{cases}

(A.1)

Set
\Gamma=\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n};\,0<|h_{y}|<\mbox{$\frac{|x-z|}{2}-|s_{y}|$}\}
,
\Gamma_{+}=\{y\in\Gamma;\,s_{y}\geq 0\}
,
\Gamma_{-}=\{y\in\Gamma;\,s_{y}<0\}
and
\tilde{\Gamma}=\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n};\,0<|\tilde{h}_{y}|<\tilde{s}_{y}\}
. It is elementary to show that

|E_{xyyz}|\sim\begin{cases}\frac{|h_{y}|}{\min\{|x-y|,|y-z|\}},\quad&y\in\Gamma,\\
1,&y\notin\overline{\Gamma},\end{cases}

(A.2)

and that

|E_{ww^{\prime}xy}|\sim\mbox{$\frac{|\tilde{h}_{y}|}{|\tilde{s}_{y}|+|\tilde{h}_{y}|}$}\sim\mbox{$\frac{|\tilde{h}_{y}|}{|x-y|}$}\sim\begin{cases}\mbox{$\frac{|\tilde{h}_{y}|}{\tilde{s}_{y}}$},\quad&y\in\tilde{\Gamma},\\
1,&y\notin\overline{\tilde{\Gamma}}.\end{cases}

(A.3)

We first prove the special case
q=0
of Proposition
2.4
, that is the case where only one line singularity shows up.

Lemma A.1
.

Suppose
n\geq 2
,
k_{1},l_{1}\in[0,n)
,
k_{2},l_{2}\in[0,+\infty)
,
\beta\in(0,+\infty)
,
k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta\geq n
, and
p\in[0,n-1)
. It follows uniformly in
y_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
that

\begin{split}&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\frac{\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}}{\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}|E_{xyyz}|^{p}}{\rm d}y\\
\lesssim&\begin{cases}\langle|x-z|^{-\max\{0,k_{1}+l_{1}-n\}}\rangle\langle x-z\rangle^{-\min\{k_{2},l_{2},k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta-n,k_{2}+l_{2}-p\}},\quad&k_{1}+l_{1}\neq n,\\
\langle|x-z|^{0-}\rangle\langle x-z\rangle^{-\min\{k_{2},l_{2},k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta-n,k_{2}+l_{2}-p\}},&k_{1}+l_{1}=n.\end{cases}\end{split}

(A.4)

Proof.

By (
A.2
) and Lemma
2.3
, the estimate for the integral over
\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus\Gamma
immediately follows. Now consider the integral over
\Gamma_{+}
. If
y\in\Gamma_{+}
, we have

\begin{cases}|x-y|\sim\frac{|x-z|}{2}-s_{y}\lesssim|x-z|,\\
|y-z|\sim|x-z|,\\
|E_{xyyz}|\sim\frac{|h_{y}|}{|x-y|},\\
\langle y-y_{0}\rangle\sim\langle s_{y}-s_{y_{0}}\rangle+\langle h_{y}-h_{y_{0}}\rangle.\end{cases}

(A.5)

When
|x-z|\leq 2
, since
\langle x-y\rangle\sim\langle y-z\rangle\sim 1
,
p<n-1
and
k_{1}<n
, we have

\begin{split}\int_{\Gamma_{+}}\frac{\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}{\rm d}y}{|x-y|^{k_{1}}|y-z|^{l_{1}}|E_{xyyz}|^{p}}\lesssim&|x-z|^{-l_{1}}\int_{0}^{\frac{|x-z|}{2}}\left(\mbox{$\frac{|x-z|}{2}-s_{y}$}\right)^{-(k_{1}-p)}\left(\int_{|h_{y}|<\frac{|x-z|}{2}-s_{y}}|h_{y}|^{-p}{\rm d}h_{y}\right){\rm d}s_{y}\\
\lesssim&|x-z|^{-(k_{1}+l_{1}-n)}.\end{split}

(A.6)

When
|x-z|>2
and
y\in\Gamma_{+}
,
\langle|y-z|^{-1}\rangle\sim 1
and
\langle y-z\rangle\sim|y-z|
follow. Set
\theta=\min\{k_{2},l_{2},k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta-n\}\geq 0
, we have

\frac{1}{|x-y|^{k_{2}-p}|y-z|^{l_{2}}}\lesssim\frac{1}{|x-z|^{\theta}|x-y|^{k_{2}+l_{2}-p-\theta}},

and consequently we derive from (
A.5
) that

\begin{split}&\int_{\Gamma_{+}}\frac{\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}}{\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}|E_{xyyz}|^{p}}{\rm d}y\\
\lesssim&|x-z|^{-\theta}\int_{0}^{\frac{|x-z|}{2}-1}\left(\mbox{$\frac{|x-z|}{2}-s_{y}$}\right)^{-(k_{2}+l_{2}-p-\theta)}\left(\int_{|h_{y}|<\frac{|x-z|}{2}-s_{y}}\frac{\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}{\rm d}h_{y}}{|h_{y}|^{p}}\right){\rm d}s_{y}\\
&+|x-z|^{-l_{2}}\int_{\frac{|x-z|}{2}-1}^{\frac{|x-z|}{2}}\left(\mbox{$\frac{|x-z|}{2}-s_{y}$}\right)^{-(k_{1}-p)}\left(\int_{|h_{y}|<\frac{|x-z|}{2}-s_{y}}\frac{\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}{\rm d}h_{y}}{|h_{y}|^{p}}\right){\rm d}s_{y},\end{split}

(A.7)

where the second term on the RHS is bounded by
|x-z|^{-l_{2}}
if we neglect
\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}
, so we are left to show

\int_{0}^{\frac{|x-z|}{2}-1}\left(\mbox{$\frac{|x-z|}{2}-s_{y}$}\right)^{-(k_{2}+l_{2}-p-\theta)}\left(\int_{|h_{y}|<\frac{|x-z|}{2}-s_{y}}\frac{\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}{\rm d}h_{y}}{|h_{y}|^{p}}\right){\rm d}s_{y}\lesssim|x-z|^{-\min\{\theta,k_{2}+l_{2}-p\}}.

If
k_{2}+l_{2}-p\leq\theta
which implies
\beta\geq n-p
, since

\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}\lesssim\langle s_{y}-s_{y_{0}}\rangle^{-(\beta-(n-p)+1)-}\langle h_{y}-h_{y_{0}}\rangle^{-(n-1-p)-},

by (
A.5
) we have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\frac{|x-z|}{2}-1}\left(\mbox{$\frac{|x-z|}{2}-s_{y}$}\right)^{-(k_{2}+l_{2}-p-\theta)}\left(\int_{|h_{y}|<\frac{|x-z|}{2}-s_{y}}\frac{\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}{\rm d}h_{y}}{|h_{y}|^{p}}\right){\rm d}s_{y}\\
\lesssim&|x-z|^{\theta-(k_{2}+l_{2}-p)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\langle s_{y}-s_{y_{0}}\rangle^{-(1+\beta-(n-p))-}{\rm d}s_{y}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}}\frac{\langle h_{y}-h_{y_{0}}\rangle^{-(n-1-p)-}}{|h_{y}|^{p}}{\rm d}h_{y}\\
\lesssim&|x-z|^{\theta-(k_{2}+l_{2}-p)}.\end{split}

If
k_{2}+l_{2}-p>\theta
, we use

\begin{split}&\left(\mbox{$\frac{|x-z|}{2}-s_{y}$}\right)^{-(k_{2}+l_{2}-p-\theta)}\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}\\
\lesssim&\left(\mbox{$\frac{|x-z|}{2}-s_{y}$}\right)^{-(k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta-\theta-p)-}+\langle s_{y}-s_{y_{0}}\rangle^{-(k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta-n-\theta+1)-}\langle h_{y}-h_{y_{0}}\rangle^{-(n-1-p)-},\end{split}

to get

\int_{0}^{\frac{|x-z|}{2}-1}\left(\mbox{$\frac{|x-z|}{2}-s_{y}$}\right)^{-(k_{2}+l_{2}-p-\theta)}\left(\int_{|h_{y}|<\frac{|x-z|}{2}-s_{y}}\frac{\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}{\rm d}h_{y}}{|h_{y}|^{p}}\right){\rm d}s_{y}\lesssim 1.

Now the estimate for the integral over
\Gamma_{+}
is shown, and the part over
\Gamma_{-}
follows in a parallel way.
∎

Now we turn to prove Proposition
2.4
.

Proof of Proposition
2.4
.

We break the integral into three parts.

Part 1: Integral over
\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus\tilde{\Gamma}

|E_{ww^{\prime}xy}|\sim 1
when
y\in\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus\tilde{\Gamma}
, so (
A.4
) implies the desired bound for the integral over
\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus\tilde{\Gamma}
.

Part 2: Integral over
\tilde{\Gamma}\setminus\Gamma

If
y\in\tilde{\Gamma}\setminus\Gamma
, then
|E_{xyyz}|\sim 1
,
|x-y|\sim\tilde{s}_{y}
,
|E_{ww^{\prime}xy}|\sim\frac{|\tilde{h}_{y}|}{\tilde{s}_{y}}
, and

\begin{split}&\int_{\tilde{\Gamma}\setminus\Gamma}\frac{\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}}{\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}|E_{xyyz}|^{p}|E_{ww^{\prime}xy}|^{q}}{\rm d}y\\
\lesssim&\int_{\tilde{\Gamma}}\frac{\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}\langle\tilde{s}_{y}^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle\tilde{s}_{y}\rangle^{-k_{2}}\tilde{s}_{y}^{q}\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-z\rangle^{-l_{2}}}{|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{q}}{\rm d}y.\end{split}

(A.8)

We split the RHS of (
A.8
) into 4 parts corresponded to the integration over

\begin{split}&A=\left\{y\in\tilde{\Gamma};\,\tilde{s}_{y}\geq 2|x-z|\right\},\\
&B=\left\{y\in\tilde{\Gamma};\,0<\tilde{s}_{y}<2|x-z|,\,\mbox{$|y-z|\geq\frac{1}{2}|x-z|$}\right\},\\
&C=\left\{y\in\tilde{\Gamma};\,0<\tilde{s}_{y}<2|x-z|,\,\mbox{$|y-z|<\frac{1}{2}|x-z|$},\,\mbox{$|\tilde{h}_{y}|\geq\frac{1}{2}|\tilde{h}_{z}|$}\right\},\\
&D=\left\{y\in\tilde{\Gamma};\,0<\tilde{s}_{y}<2|x-z|,\,\mbox{$|y-z|<\frac{1}{2}|x-z|$},\,\mbox{$|\tilde{h}_{y}|<\frac{1}{2}|\tilde{h}_{z}|$}\right\}.\end{split}

If
y\in A
, then
|y-z|\geq|\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{z}|\geq\tilde{s}_{y}-|x-z|\geq\frac{\tilde{s}_{y}}{2}
, and thus

\begin{split}I_{A}&\triangleq\int_{A}\frac{\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}\langle\tilde{s}_{y}^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle\tilde{s}_{y}\rangle^{-k_{2}}\tilde{s}_{y}^{q}\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-z\rangle^{-l_{2}}}{|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{q}}{\rm d}y\\
&\lesssim\int_{2|x-z|}^{+\infty}\langle\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{y_{0}}\rangle^{-\beta-}\langle\tilde{s}_{y}^{-(k_{1}+l_{1})}\rangle\langle\tilde{s}_{y}\rangle^{-(k_{2}+l_{2})}\tilde{s}_{y}^{q}\left(\int_{|\tilde{h}_{y}|<\tilde{s}_{y}}\frac{{\rm d}\tilde{h}_{y}}{|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{q}}\right){\rm d}\tilde{s}_{y}\\
&\sim\int_{2|x-z|}^{+\infty}\langle\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{y_{0}}\rangle^{-\beta-}\langle\tilde{s}_{y}^{-(k_{1}+l_{1})}\rangle\langle\tilde{s}_{y}\rangle^{-(k_{2}+l_{2})}\tilde{s}_{y}^{n-1}{\rm d}\tilde{s}_{y}.\end{split}

It is quite elementary to get a sufficient bound

I_{A}\lesssim\begin{cases}\langle|x-z|^{-\max\{0,k_{1}+l_{1}-n\}}\rangle\langle x-z\rangle^{-(k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta-n)},\quad&k_{1}+l_{1}\neq n,\\
\langle|x-z|^{0-}\rangle\langle x-z\rangle^{-(k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta-n)},\quad&k_{1}+l_{1}=n.\end{cases}

Consider the integration over
B
. Set

I_{B}\triangleq\int_{B}\frac{\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}\langle\tilde{s}_{y}^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle\tilde{s}_{y}\rangle^{-k_{2}}\tilde{s}_{y}^{q}\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-z\rangle^{-l_{2}}}{|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{q}}{\rm d}y.

Note that when
y\in B
, we have
|y-z|\gtrsim|x-z|
,
\tilde{s}_{y}\sim|x-y|
and
|\tilde{h}_{y}|<\tilde{s}_{y}<2|x-z|
, as how we treat (
A.6
) and (
A.7
), it follows that

I_{B}\lesssim\langle|x-z|^{-(k_{1}+l_{1}-n)}\rangle\langle x-z\rangle^{-\min\{k_{2},l_{2},k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta-n,k_{2}+l_{2}-q\}}.

Consider the integration over
C
. If
y\in C
, we have
\tilde{s}_{y}\sim|x-y|\sim|x-z|
, and it follows from (
A.3
) that
|E_{ww^{\prime}xy}|\sim\frac{|\tilde{h}_{y}|}{\tilde{s}_{y}}\gtrsim\frac{|\tilde{h}_{z}|}{|x-z|}\sim|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|
. Consequently,

\begin{split}I_{C}&\triangleq\int_{C}\frac{\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}\langle\tilde{s}_{y}^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle\tilde{s}_{y}\rangle^{-k_{2}}\tilde{s}_{y}^{q}\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-z\rangle^{-l_{2}}}{|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{q}}{\rm d}y\\
&\sim\int_{C}\frac{\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}}{\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}|E_{ww^{\prime}xy}|^{q}}{\rm d}y\\
&\lesssim|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\frac{\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}}{\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}}{\rm d}y\end{split}

(A.9)

and the estimate for
I_{C}
immediately follows from Lemma
2.3
.

Consider the integration over
D
. If
y\in D
, we have
\tilde{s}_{y}\sim|x-y|\sim|x-z|
and
|y-z|\geq|\tilde{h}_{y}-\tilde{h}_{z}|\sim|\tilde{h}_{z}|
. Set

I_{D}\triangleq\int_{D}\frac{\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}\langle\tilde{s}_{y}^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle\tilde{s}_{y}\rangle^{-k_{2}}\tilde{s}_{y}^{q}\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-z\rangle^{-l_{2}}}{|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{q}}{\rm d}y.

(A.10)

When
|x-z|\lesssim 1
, first observe that
|\tilde{h}_{z}|\sim|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}||x-z|\lesssim 1
and
|y-z|<\frac{1}{2}|x-z|\lesssim 1
hold for
y\in D
. If
0\leq l_{1}\leq n-1
, we deduce

\begin{split}I_{D}&\lesssim\int_{\tilde{s}_{y}\sim|x-z|}\frac{1}{\tilde{s}_{y}^{k_{1}-q}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{l_{1}}}\left(\int_{|\tilde{h}_{y}|<\frac{1}{2}|\tilde{h}_{z}|}\frac{{\rm d}\tilde{h}_{y}}{|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{q}}\right){\rm d}\tilde{s}_{y}\\
&\sim|x-z|^{-(k_{1}-q-1)}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{-(l_{1}+q+1-n)}\\
&\sim|x-z|^{-(k_{1}+l_{1}-n)}|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{n-1-l_{1}}\\
&\leq|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-(k_{1}+l_{1}-n)},\end{split}

and if
n-1<l_{1}<n
, we use
1\gtrsim|x-z|^{l_{1}}\gtrsim|y-z|^{l_{1}}\gtrsim|\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{z}|^{l_{1}-(n-1)}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{n-1}
when
y\in D
to deduce

\begin{split}I_{D}&\lesssim|x-z|^{-(k_{1}-q)}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{-(n-1)}\int_{\tilde{s}_{y}\sim|x-z|}\frac{1}{|\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{z}|^{l_{1}-(n-1)}}\left(\int_{|\tilde{h}_{y}|<\frac{1}{2}|\tilde{h}_{z}|}\frac{{\rm d}\tilde{h}_{y}}{|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{q}}\right){\rm d}\tilde{s}_{y}\\
&\lesssim|x-z|^{-(k_{1}+l_{1}-n-q)}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{-q}\\
&\sim|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-(k_{1}+l_{1}-n)}.\end{split}

When
|x-z|\gtrsim 1
, we decompose
I_{D}
into
I_{D_{1}}
and
I_{D_{2}}
with respect to the region of integration, where
D_{1}=D\cap\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n};\,|y-z|<1\}
and
D_{2}=D\cap\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n};\,|y-z|\geq 1\}
. Recall
|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|\sim\frac{|\tilde{h}_{z}|}{|\tilde{s}_{y}|}
holds when
y\in D
, we first see that
I_{D_{1}}
has the bound

\begin{split}I_{D_{1}}\lesssim&\int_{D\cap\{|y-z|<1\}}\frac{{\rm d}y}{\tilde{s}_{y}^{k_{2}-q}|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{q}|y-z|^{l_{1}}}\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-k_{2}}\int_{D\cap\{|y-z|<1\}}\frac{|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{q}{\rm d}y}{|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{q}|y-z|^{l_{1}}},\end{split}

so if
0\leq l_{1}\leq n-1
, we use the facts that
|y-z|\gtrsim|\tilde{h}_{z}|
and
|\tilde{h}_{z}|\sim|\tilde{h}_{y}-\tilde{h}_{z}|\leq|y-z|<1
when
y\in D_{1}
to get

\begin{split}I_{D_{1}}\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-k_{2}}\int_{D\cap\{|y-z|<1\}}\frac{|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{n-1-l_{1}}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{-(n-1-q)}}{|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{q}}{\rm d}y\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-k_{2}}\int_{|\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{z}|<1}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{-(n-1-q)}\left(\int_{|\tilde{h}_{y}|<\frac{1}{2}|\tilde{h}_{z}|}\frac{{\rm d}\tilde{h}_{y}}{|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{q}}\right){\rm d}\tilde{s}_{y}\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-k_{2}},\end{split}

while in the case of
n-1<l_{1}<n
, we use
|y-z|^{l}\gtrsim|\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{z}|^{l_{1}-(n-1)}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{n-1}
to get

\begin{split}I_{D_{1}}\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-k_{2}}\int_{|\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{z}|<1}|\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{z}|^{n-1-l_{1}}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{-(n-1-q)}\left(\int_{|\tilde{h}_{y}|<\frac{1}{2}|\tilde{h}_{z}|}\frac{{\rm d}\tilde{h}_{y}}{|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{q}}\right){\rm d}\tilde{s}_{y}\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-k_{2}}.\end{split}

For
I_{D_{2}}
, recall
|\tilde{h}_{z}|\leq|x-z|\sim\tilde{s}_{y}
holds when
y\in D
, we have

\begin{split}I_{D_{2}}\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-(k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta-n)}\int_{D\cap\{|y-z|\geq 1\}}\frac{\langle\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{y_{0}}\rangle^{-\beta-}\langle\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{z}\rangle^{-l_{2}}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{n-1}}{\tilde{s}_{y}^{n-l_{2}-\beta}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{n-1-q}|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{q}}{\rm d}y\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-(k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta-n)}\int_{\tilde{s}_{y}\sim|x-z|}\frac{\langle\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{y_{0}}\rangle^{-\beta-}\langle\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{z}\rangle^{-l_{2}}}{\tilde{s}_{y}^{1-l_{2}-\beta}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{n-1-q}}\left(\int_{|\tilde{h}_{y}|<\frac{1}{2}|\tilde{h}_{z}|}\frac{{\rm d}\tilde{h}_{y}}{|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{q}}\right){\rm d}\tilde{s}_{y}\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-(k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta-n)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\langle\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{y_{0}}\rangle^{-\beta-}\langle\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{z}\rangle^{-l_{2}}\langle\tilde{s}_{y}\rangle^{-(1-l_{2}-\beta)}{\rm d}\tilde{s}_{y}\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-(k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta-n)}.\end{split}

(A.11)

Now it is clear that

I_{D}\lesssim|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}\langle|x-z|^{-(k_{1}+l_{1}-n)}\rangle\langle x-z\rangle^{-\min\{k_{2},k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta-n\}}.

and the bound for (
A.8
) has been completely shown.

Part 3. Integral over
\tilde{\Gamma}\cap\Gamma

A routine calculation combining (
A.1
) and
z=x+\tilde{s}_{z}\frac{(w^{\prime}-w)}{|w^{\prime}-w|}+\tilde{h}_{z}
shows that

\tilde{h}_{y}-h_{y}=\left(\left(\mbox{$\frac{|x-z|}{2}-s_{y}$}\right)\mbox{$\frac{\tilde{s}_{z}}{|x-z|}$}-\tilde{s}_{y}\right)\mbox{$\frac{(w^{\prime}-w)}{|w^{\prime}-w|}$}+\left(\mbox{$\frac{|x-z|}{2}-s_{y}$}\right)\mbox{$\frac{\tilde{h}_{z}}{|x-z|}$}.

If
y\in\tilde{\Gamma}\cap\Gamma
, we have
|\tilde{h}_{y}-h_{y}|\geq(\frac{|x-z|}{2}-s_{y})\frac{|\tilde{h}_{z}|}{|x-z|}\sim|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}||x-y|

and therefore

\begin{split}\frac{1}{|h_{y}|^{p}|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{q}}&\lesssim|\tilde{h}_{y}-h_{y}|^{-\min\{p,q\}}\left(|h_{y}|^{-\max\{p,q\}}+|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{-\max\{p,q\}}\right)\\
&\lesssim|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-\min\{p,q\}}|x-y|^{-\min\{p,q\}}\left(|h_{y}|^{-\max\{p,q\}}+|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{-\max\{p,q\}}\right).\end{split}

(A.12)

We first consider the integral over
\tilde{\Gamma}\cap\Gamma_{+}
, where
\min\{|x-y|,|y-z|\}=|x-y|
and
|y-z|\sim|x-z|
. Then

\begin{split}&\int_{\tilde{\Gamma}\cap\Gamma_{+}}\frac{\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}}{\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}|E_{xyyz}|^{p}|E_{ww^{\prime}xy}|^{q}}{\rm d}y\\
\lesssim&\int_{\tilde{\Gamma}\cap\Gamma_{+}}\frac{\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle}{|x-y|^{-(p+q)}\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}|h_{y}|^{p}|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{q}}{\rm d}y\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-\min\{p,q\}}\int_{\tilde{\Gamma}\cap\Gamma_{+}}\frac{\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\left(|h_{y}|^{-\max\{p,q\}}+|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{-\max\{p,q\}}\right)}{|x-y|^{-\max\{p,q\}}\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}}{\rm d}y.\end{split}

Since when
y\in\tilde{\Gamma}\cap\Gamma_{+}
, we have
|h_{y}|<\frac{|x-z|}{2}-s_{y}<\frac{|x-z|}{2}
, and
|\tilde{h}_{y}|<\tilde{s}_{y}<|x-z|
, as how we treat (
A.6
) and (
A.7
), it follows that

\begin{split}&\int_{\tilde{\Gamma}\cap\Gamma_{+}}\frac{\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}}{\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}|E_{xyyz}|^{p}|E_{ww^{\prime}xy}|^{q}}{\rm d}y\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-\min\{p,q\}}\langle|x-z|^{-(k_{1}+l_{1}-n)}\rangle\langle x-z\rangle^{-\min\{k_{2},l_{2},k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta-n,k_{2}+l_{2}-\max\{p,q\}\}}.\end{split}

Finally consider the integral over
\tilde{\Gamma}\cap\Gamma_{-}
, where
\min\{|x-y|,|y-z|\}=|y-z|\sim\frac{|x-z|}{2}+s_{y}
,
|x-y|\sim\tilde{s}_{y}\sim|x-z|
. We split
\tilde{\Gamma}\cap\Gamma_{-}
into

\begin{split}E=\left\{y\in\tilde{\Gamma}\cap\Gamma_{-};~|\tilde{h}_{y}|\geq\mbox{$\frac{1}{2}$}|\tilde{h}_{z}|\right\},\quad F=\left\{y\in\tilde{\Gamma}\cap\Gamma_{-};~|\tilde{h}_{y}|<\mbox{$\frac{1}{2}$}|\tilde{h}_{z}|\right\},\end{split}

Similar to (
A.9
), we immediately get

\begin{split}&\int_{E}\frac{\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}}{\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}|E_{xyyz}|^{p}|E_{ww^{\prime}xy}|^{q}}{\rm d}y\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}\int_{E}\frac{\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}}{\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}|E_{xyyz}|^{p}}{\rm d}y\\
\lesssim&\begin{cases}|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}\langle|x-z|^{-\max\{0,k_{1}+l_{1}-n\}}\rangle\langle x-z\rangle^{-\min\{k_{2},l_{2},k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta-n,k_{2}+l_{2}-p\}},&k_{1}+l_{1}\neq n,\\
|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}\langle|x-z|^{0-}\rangle\langle x-z\rangle^{-\min\{k_{2},l_{2},k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta-n,k_{2}+l_{2}-p\}},&k_{1}+l_{1}=n.\end{cases}\end{split}

On the other hand, when
y\in F
, note that
|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|\sim\frac{|\tilde{h}_{z}|}{|x-z|}\sim\frac{|\tilde{h}_{z}|}{|x-y|}
, (
A.12
) also says

\frac{1}{|h_{y}|^{p}|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{q}}\lesssim|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}\frac{|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{q-\min\{p,q\}}}{|x-y|^{q}}\left(|h_{y}|^{-\max\{p,q\}}+|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{-\max\{p,q\}}\right),

and thus

\begin{split}&\int_{F}\frac{\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}}{\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}|E_{xyyz}|^{p}|E_{ww^{\prime}xy}|^{q}}{\rm d}y\\
\lesssim&\int_{F}\frac{\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}}{|x-y|^{-q}|y-z|^{-p}\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}|h_{y}|^{p}|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{q}}{\rm d}y\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}\int_{F}\frac{|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{q-\min\{p,q\}}\left(|h_{y}|^{-\max\{p,q\}}+|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{-\max\{p,q\}}\right)\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}}{\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}|y-z|^{-p}}{\rm d}y\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}\int_{F}\frac{\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}}{\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}|y-z|^{-\max\{p,q\}}|h_{y}|^{\max\{p,q\}}}{\rm d}y\\
&+|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}\int_{F}\frac{|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{q-\min\{p,q\}}\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}}{\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}|y-z|^{-p}|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{\max\{p,q\}}}{\rm d}y,\end{split}

(A.13)

where we have used
|\tilde{h}_{z}|\sim|\tilde{h}_{y}-\tilde{h}_{z}|\leq|y-z|
for the first term on the RHS of (
A.13
). Since
|h_{y}|<\frac{|x-z|}{2}+s_{y}<\frac{|x-z|}{2}
for
y\in F
, as how we treat (
A.6
) and (
A.7
), it again follows that

\begin{split}&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}\int_{F}\frac{\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}}{\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}|E_{xyyz}|^{p}|E_{ww^{\prime}xy}|^{q}}{\rm d}y\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}\langle|x-z|^{-(k_{1}+l_{1}-n)}\rangle\langle x-z\rangle^{-\min\{k_{2},l_{2},k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta-n,k_{2}+l_{2}-\max\{p,q\}\}}.\end{split}

Estimating the last term on the RHS of (
A.13
) is not parallel, but more like mimicking the treatment for (
A.10
). When
|x-z|\lesssim 1
, which implies
|x-y|\sim\tilde{s}_{y}\lesssim 1
,
|y-z|\leq\frac{|x-z|}{2}\lesssim 1
,
\langle|x-y|^{-1}\rangle\sim|x-z|^{-1}
and
\langle x-y\rangle\sim\langle y-z\rangle\sim 1
when
y\in F
, we first have

\begin{split}&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}\int_{F}\frac{|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{q-\min\{p,q\}}\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}}{\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}|y-z|^{-p}|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{\max\{p,q\}}}{\rm d}y\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-k_{1}}\int_{F}\frac{\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{q-\min\{p,q\}}}{|y-z|^{l_{1}-p}|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{\max\{p,q\}}}{\rm d}y,\end{split}

(A.14)

and it is easy to check when
y\in F
that

\frac{1}{|y-z|^{l_{1}-p}}\lesssim\begin{cases}|x-z|^{p-l_{1}},\quad&0\leq l_{1}\leq p,\\
|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{p-l_{1}},&p<l_{1}\leq n-1,\\
\frac{1}{|\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{z}|^{l_{1}-(n-1)}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{n-1-p}},&p<n-1<l_{1}<n,\end{cases}

(A.15)

so putting (
A.15
) into (
A.14
) and using
|\tilde{h}_{z}|\leq|x-z|
, one easily obtains

\begin{split}&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}\int_{F}\frac{|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{q-\min\{p,q\}}\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}}{\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}|y-z|^{-p}|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{\max\{p,q\}}}{\rm d}y\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-k_{1}}\int_{\{\tilde{s}_{y}\sim|x-z|\}\cap\{|\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{z}|\lesssim 1\}}\left(\int_{|\tilde{h}_{y}|<\frac{1}{2}|\tilde{h}_{z}|}\frac{|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{q-\min\{p,q\}}{\rm d}\tilde{h}_{y}}{|y-z|^{l_{1}-p}|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{\max\{p,q\}}}\right){\rm d}\tilde{s}_{y}\\
\lesssim&\begin{cases}|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-(k_{1}-\max\{0,p-l_{1}\}-1)}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{n-1-\max\{p,l_{1}\}},\quad&0\leq l_{1}\leq n-1,\\
|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-(k_{1}+l_{1}-n)},&n-1<l_{1}<n,\end{cases}\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-(k_{1}+l_{1}-n)}.\end{split}

When
|x-z|\gtrsim 1
, similar to the treatment for
I_{D}
above by considering
I_{D_{1}}
and
I_{D_{2}}
, we first observe that if
y\in F\cap\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n};\,|y-z|<1\}
, it follows that
\langle|x-y|^{-1}\rangle\sim\langle y-z\rangle\sim 1
,
\langle x-y\rangle\sim|x-z|
,
|\tilde{h}_{z}|\lesssim|\tilde{h}_{y}-\tilde{h}_{z}|<1
, and

\frac{1}{|y-z|^{l_{1}-p}}\lesssim\begin{cases}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{\min\{0,p-l_{1}\}},&0\leq l_{1}\leq n-1,\\
\frac{1}{|\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{z}|^{l_{1}-(n-1)}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{n-1-p}},&n-1<l_{1}<n,\end{cases}

so we may first deduce

\begin{split}&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}\int_{F\cap\{|y-z|<1\}}\frac{|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{q-\min\{p,q\}}\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}}{\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}|y-z|^{-p}|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{\max\{p,q\}}}{\rm d}y\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-k_{2}}\int_{|\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{z}|<1}\mathbbm{1}_{\{|\tilde{h}_{z}|\lesssim 1\}}\left(\int_{|\tilde{h}_{y}|<\frac{1}{2}|\tilde{h}_{z}|}\frac{|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{q-\min\{p,q\}}{\rm d}\tilde{h}_{y}}{|y-z|^{l_{1}-p}|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{\max\{p,q\}}}\right){\rm d}\tilde{s}_{y}\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-k_{2}}.\end{split}

Now we are only left to consider the integration over
F\cap\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n};\,|y-z|<1\}
when
|x-z|\gtrsim 1
, where
\langle|x-y|^{-1}\rangle\sim\langle|y-z|^{-1}\rangle\sim 1
. Almost parallel to the discussion for
I_{D_{2}}
in (
A.11
), we have

\begin{split}&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}\int_{F\cap\{|y-z|\geq 1\}}\frac{|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{q-\min\{p,q\}}\langle|x-y|^{-k_{1}}\rangle\langle|y-z|^{-l_{1}}\rangle\langle y-y_{0}\rangle^{-\beta-}}{\langle x-y\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle y-z\rangle^{l_{2}}|y-z|^{-p}|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{\max\{p,q\}}}{\rm d}y\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-(k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta-n)}\int_{F\cap\{|y-z|\geq 1\}}\frac{\langle\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{y_{0}}\rangle^{-\beta-}|x-z|^{l_{2}+\beta-n}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{n-1-p}}{|y-z|^{l_{2}-p}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{n-1-\max\{p,q\}}|\tilde{h}_{y}|^{\max\{p,q\}}}{\rm d}y\\
\lesssim&|E_{ww^{\prime}xz}|^{-q}|x-z|^{-(k_{2}+l_{2}+\beta-n)},\end{split}

where the minor difference here is that we have used
|\tilde{h}_{z}|\lesssim|\tilde{h}_{y}-\tilde{h}_{z}|\lesssim|y-z|\lesssim|x-z|\sim\tilde{s}_{y}
and
p<n-1
to deduce for
y\in F\cap\{|y-z|\geq 1\}
that

\frac{|x-z|^{l_{2}+\beta-n}|\tilde{h}_{z}|^{n-1-p}}{|y-z|^{l_{2}-p}}\lesssim\frac{|x-z|^{l_{2}+\beta-n}|y-z|^{n-1}}{|y-z|^{l_{2}}}\lesssim\frac{|x-z|^{l_{2}+\beta-1}}{|y-z|^{l_{2}}}\lesssim\langle\tilde{s}_{y}-\tilde{s}_{z}\rangle^{-l_{2}}\langle\tilde{s}_{y}\rangle^{-(1-l_{2}-\beta)}.

Now the estimate for the integral over
\tilde{\Gamma}\cap\Gamma
has been shown, and the proof is complete.
∎

Appendix   B
The proofs of Lemma
3.1
and Lemma
3.2

B.1.
The proof of Lemma
3.1

By (
2.3
), we have

|\partial_{\lambda}^{s_{i}}R_{0}^{\pm}(\lambda^{2m})(x)|\lesssim|x|^{2m-n}+|x|^{-\frac{n-1}{2}+s_{i}},\qquad 0\leq s_{i}\leq\mbox{$\frac{n+1}{2}$}.

On the other hand, a direct computation yields

\begin{array}[]{ll}\left|\partial_{\lambda}^{l}\left(e^{{\rm i}s\lambda_{k}|x-y|\mp{\rm i}s\lambda|x|}\right)\right|=\left|\partial_{\lambda}^{l}\left(e^{{\rm i}s\lambda_{k}(|x-y|-|x|)}e^{{\rm i}s(\lambda_{k}\mp\lambda)|x|}\right)\right|\lesssim_{l}\lambda^{-l}\langle y\rangle^{l}.\end{array}

If
l>[\frac{n}{2m}]+2
, then it follows that

\begin{split}&\left|v(y)\left(\prod_{j=0}^{l-1}(R_{0}^{\pm,(s_{j})}(\lambda^{2m})V)\partial_{\lambda}^{s_{l}}\left(|x-\cdot|^{-\tau}e^{\mp{\rm i}\lambda s|x|}e^{{\rm i}\lambda_{k}s|x-y|}\right)\right)(y)\right|\\
\lesssim&\lambda^{-s_{l}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{nl}}\langle y\rangle^{-\frac{\beta}{2}}\prod_{i=0}^{l-1}\left|\partial_{\lambda}^{s_{i}}R_{0}^{\pm}(\lambda^{2m})(z_{i}-z_{i+1})V(z_{i+1})\right|\langle z_{l}\rangle^{s_{l}}|z_{l}-x|^{-\tau}dz_{1}\cdots dz_{l}\right|\\
\lesssim&\lambda^{-s_{l}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{nl}}\langle y\rangle^{-\frac{\beta}{2}-s_{0}}\prod_{i=0}^{l-1}\left((|z_{i}-z_{i+1}|^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}+|z_{i}-z_{i+1}|^{2m-n})\langle z_{i+1}\rangle^{-\beta+s_{i}+s_{i+1}}\right)|z_{l}-x|^{-\tau}{\rm d}z_{1}\cdots{\rm d}z_{l}\\
\lesssim&\lambda^{-s_{l}}\langle y\rangle^{-\frac{\beta}{2}+s_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}|y-z|^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\langle z\rangle^{-\beta+s_{l-1}+s_{l}}|z-x|^{-\tau}{\rm d}z,\end{split}

(B.1)

where
z_{0}=y
,
s_{0}+\cdots+s_{l}\leq\frac{n+1}{2}
and the last inequality follows by repeatedly using the following estimate

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\big(|x-z|^{-\max\{n-2mj,\,\frac{n-1}{2}\}}

\displaystyle+|x-z|^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\big)\langle z\rangle^{-\frac{n+3}{2}}\big(|x-z|^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}+|z-y|^{2m-n}\big)dz

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|x-z|^{-\max\{n-2m(j+1),\,\frac{n-1}{2}\}}+|x-z|^{-\frac{n-1}{2}},\quad\mbox{if}\,\,\,2mj<n,

which, in turn, follows from Lemma
2.3
, the fact that
\beta-s_{i}-s_{i+1}>\frac{n+3}{2}
, and the inequality

\langle z_{i}\rangle^{-s_{i}}\langle z_{i+1}\rangle^{-s_{i}}\left(|z_{i}-z_{i+1}|^{-\frac{n-1}{2}+s_{i}}+|z_{i}-z_{i+1}|^{2m-n}\right)\leq|z_{i}-z_{i+1}|^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}+|z_{i}-z_{i+1}|^{2m-n}.

Therefore, the Minkowski’s inequality implies that the
L^{2}
norm of the right hand side of (
B.1
) is controlled by

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\left\|\langle y\rangle^{-\frac{\beta}{2}+s_{0}}|y-z|^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}\right\|_{L^{2}_{y}}\langle z\rangle^{-\beta+s_{l-1}+s_{l}}|z-x|^{-\tau}{\rm d}z

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\langle z\rangle^{-\beta+s_{l-1}+s_{l}+\max\{-\frac{\beta}{2}+s_{0},-\frac{n-1}{2}\}}|z-x|^{-\tau}{\rm d}z\lesssim\langle x\rangle^{-\tau},

where we have used Lemma
2.2
, as well as the facts
\frac{\beta}{2}-s_{0}>\frac{1}{2}
and
-\beta+s_{l-1}+s_{l}+\max\{\frac{1-\beta}{2}+s_{0},-\frac{n-1}{2}\}<-n
when
\beta>n+2
.
Therefore (
3.7
) follows.

In order to prove (
3.8
), first note that (
2.2
) implies

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{\lambda}^{s_{j}}\left(R_{0}^{+}(\lambda^{2m})(x-y)-R_{0}^{-}(\lambda^{2m})(x-y)\right)\right|\lesssim\sum_{s_{j,1}+s_{j,2}=s_{j}}\lambda^{n-2m-s_{j,1}}|x-y|^{s_{j,2}}\lesssim\lambda^{n-2m-s_{j}},

provided
\lambda|x-y|\leq 1
.
On the other hand, by (
2.1
), we derive when
\lambda|x-y|\geq 1
and
0<\lambda<1
that

\displaystyle\left|\langle y\rangle^{-1}\langle x\rangle^{-1}\partial_{\lambda}^{s_{j}}R_{0}^{\pm}(\lambda^{2m})(x-y)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{0\leq j\leq{\frac{n-3}{2}}}\sum_{s_{j,1}+s_{j,2}=s_{j}}{\lambda^{j+2-2m-s_{j,1}}|x-y|^{-(n-2-j)+s_{j,2}}\langle y\rangle^{-1}\langle x\rangle^{-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda^{n-2m-s_{j}}.

Combining the above two inequalities, we obtain

\left|\langle y\rangle^{-1}\langle x\rangle^{-1}\partial_{\lambda}^{s_{j}}\left(R_{0}^{+}(\lambda^{2m})(x-y)-R_{0}^{-}(\lambda^{2m})(x-y)\right)\right|\lesssim\lambda^{n-2m-s_{j}},\qquad 0<\lambda<1.

Thus the LHS of (
3.8
) is bounded by

\displaystyle\lambda^{n-2m-s_{j}}\left\|\langle z_{0}\rangle^{-\frac{\beta}{2}-s_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{nl}}\langle z_{j}\rangle^{-s_{j}+1}\langle z_{j+1}\rangle^{-\beta+s_{j}+1}\right.

\displaystyle\times\left.\prod_{i\neq j,0\leq i\leq l-1}\left((|z_{i}-z_{i+1}|^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}+|z_{i}-z_{i+1}|^{2m-n})\langle z_{i+1}\rangle^{-\beta+s_{i}+s_{i+1}}\right)|z_{l}-x|^{-\tau}{\rm d}z_{1}\cdots{\rm d}z_{l}\right\|_{L_{z_{0}}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lambda^{n-2m-s_{j}},

where we have used
\beta-s_{j-1}-1>\frac{n+1}{2}
and
\beta-s_{i}-s_{i+1}>\frac{n+1}{2}
, and (
3.8
) is now proved.

B.2.
The proof of Lemma
3.2

Recall that by (
2.2
) and (
2.3
), one has

\displaystyle R_{0}^{+}(\lambda^{2m})(x-y)-R_{0}^{-}(\lambda^{2m})(x-y)=\int_{0}^{1}{\tilde{r}^{+}_{0}(\lambda,s,x-y)-\tilde{r}^{-}_{0}(\lambda,s,x-y){\rm d}s},

(B.2)

and

R_{0}^{\pm}(\lambda^{2m})(x-y)=\int_{0}^{1}{\tilde{r}^{\pm}_{2m-n,1}(\lambda,s,x-y){\rm d}s}+r^{\pm}_{2m-n,0}(\lambda,x-y),

(B.3)

where

\tilde{r}^{\pm}_{0}(\lambda,s,x-y)=\sum_{k\in I^{\pm}}\sum_{j=0}^{\frac{n-3}{2}}C_{j,0}\lambda_{k}^{n-2m}e^{{\rm i}s\lambda_{k}|x-y|}(1-s)^{n-j-3},

\tilde{r}^{\pm}_{2m-n,1}(\lambda,s,x-y)=\sum_{k\in I^{\pm}}|x-y|^{2m-n}\left(\sum_{l=0}^{2m-3}C_{l,2m-n}e^{{\rm i}s\lambda_{k}|x-y|}(1-s)^{2m-3-l}\right),

and

r^{\pm}_{2m-n,0}(\lambda,x-y)=\sum\limits_{k\in I^{\pm}}\sum\limits_{j=2m-2}^{\frac{n-3}{2}}D_{j}\lambda_{k}^{j+2-2m}|x-y|^{j+2-n}e^{{\rm i}\lambda_{k}|x-y|}.

We first prove (
3.9
) and (
3.10
). Set

\begin{cases}\omega_{1,1}^{\pm}(\lambda,s,y,x)=e^{\mp{\rm i}\lambda s|x|}v(y)\left((R_{0}^{\pm}(\lambda^{2m})V)^{l}\tilde{r}^{\pm}_{2m-n,1}(\lambda,s,x-\cdot)\right)(y),\\[7.11317pt]
\omega_{1,0}^{\pm}(\lambda,s,y,x)=e^{\mp{\rm i}\lambda|x|}v(y)\left((R_{0}^{\pm}(\lambda^{2m})V)^{l}r^{\pm}_{2m-n,0}(\lambda,x-\cdot)\right)(y).\end{cases}

(
3.9
) follows from (
B.3
) and the fact that
\omega_{1,0}^{\pm}(\lambda,s,y,x)
is actually independent of
s
. To obtain (
3.10
), a direct computation yields that for each
0\leq\gamma\leq\frac{n+1}{2}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\partial_{\lambda}^{\gamma}\omega_{1,1}^{\pm}(\lambda,s,\cdot,x)\right\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{0}+\cdots+s_{l}=\gamma}\sum_{k\in I^{\pm}}\sum_{j=0}^{2m-3}\left\|v(y)\left(\prod_{j=0}^{l-1}(R_{0}^{\pm,(s_{j})}(\lambda^{2m})V)\partial_{\lambda}^{s_{l}}\left(|x-\cdot|^{2m-n}e^{\mp{\rm i}\lambda s|x|}e^{{\rm i}\lambda_{k}s|x-\cdot|}\right)\right)(y)\right\|_{L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lambda^{-\gamma}\langle x\rangle^{-\frac{n-1}{2}},

where we have used (
3.7
) and the fact
\frac{n-1}{2}\leq n-2m
in the last inequality.
Similarly,

\displaystyle\left\|\partial_{\lambda}^{\gamma}\omega_{1,0}^{\pm}(\lambda,s,\cdot,x)\right\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{0}+\cdots+s_{l}=\gamma}\sum_{k\in I^{\pm}}\sum_{j=2m-2}^{\frac{n-3}{2}}\left\|v(y)\left(\prod_{j=0}^{l-1}(R_{0}^{\pm,(s_{j})}(\lambda^{2m})V)\partial_{\lambda}^{s_{l}}\left(\lambda_{k}^{j+2-2m}|x-\cdot|^{j+2-n}e^{\mp{\rm i}\lambda s|x|}e^{{\rm i}\lambda_{k}s|x-\cdot|}\right)\right)(y)\right\|_{L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lambda^{-\gamma}\langle x\rangle^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}.

These two estimates give (
3.10
) immediately.

We next prove (
3.11
)-(
3.13
). Note that

\displaystyle v(y)\left((R_{0}^{+}(\lambda^{2m})V)^{l}R_{0}^{+}(\lambda^{2m})(x-y)-(R_{0}^{-}(\lambda^{2m})V)^{l}R_{0}^{-}(\lambda^{2m})(x-y)\right)

(B.4)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle v(y)\sum_{j=0}^{l-1}(R_{0}^{-}(\lambda^{2m})V)^{j}(R_{0}^{+}(\lambda^{2m})-R_{0}^{-}(\lambda^{2m}))V(R_{0}^{+}(\lambda^{2m})V)^{l-j-1}R_{0}^{+}(\lambda^{2m})(x-y)

\displaystyle+v(y)R_{0}^{-}(\lambda^{2m})V)^{l}\left(R_{0}^{+}(\lambda^{2m})(x-y)-R_{0}^{-}(\lambda^{2m})(x-y)\right).

We take
\phi\in C_{c}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R})
with
\phi(t)=1
(
|t|\leq 1
) and
\phi(t)=0
(
|t|\geq 2
), to define

\displaystyle\small\omega_{2,1}^{-}(\lambda,s,y,x)=

\displaystyle\phi(\lambda\langle x\rangle)e^{{\rm i}\lambda s|x|}v(y)\left((R_{0}^{-}(\lambda^{2m})V)^{l}\tilde{r}^{-}_{0}(\lambda,s,x-\cdot)\right)(y)

\displaystyle+(1-\phi(\lambda\langle x\rangle))e^{{\rm i}\lambda s|x|}v(y)\left((R_{0}^{-}(\lambda^{2m})V)^{l}\tilde{r}^{-}_{2m-n,1}(\lambda,s,x-\cdot)\right)(y),

\displaystyle\omega_{2,1}^{+}(\lambda,s,y,x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\phi(\lambda\langle x\rangle)e^{-{\rm i}\lambda s|x|}v(y)\left((R_{0}^{-}(\lambda^{2m})V)^{l}\tilde{r}^{+}_{0}(\lambda,s,x-\cdot)\right)(y)

\displaystyle+(1-\phi(\lambda\langle x\rangle))e^{-{\rm i}\lambda s|x|}v(y)\left((R_{0}^{-}(\lambda^{2m})V)^{l}\tilde{r}^{+}_{2m-n,1}(\lambda,s,x-\cdot)\right)(y)+e^{-{\rm i}\lambda s|x|}v(y)

\displaystyle\times\sum_{r=0}^{l-1}\left((R_{0}^{-}(\lambda^{2m})V)^{r}(R_{0}^{+}(\lambda^{2m})-R_{0}^{-}(\lambda^{2m}))V(R_{0}^{+}(\lambda^{2m})V)^{l-r-1}\tilde{r}^{+}_{2m-n,1}(\lambda,s,x-\cdot)\right)(y),

\displaystyle\small\omega_{2,0}^{-}(\lambda,s,y,x)=(1-\phi(\lambda\langle x\rangle))e^{{\rm i}\lambda|x|}v(y)\left((R_{0}^{-}(\lambda^{2m})V)^{l}r^{-}_{2m-n,0}(\lambda,x-\cdot)\right)(y),

and

\displaystyle\omega_{2,0}^{+}(\lambda,s,y,x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\phi(\lambda\langle x\rangle))e^{-{\rm i}\lambda|x|}v(y)\left((R_{0}^{-}(\lambda^{2m})V)^{l}r^{+}_{2m-n,0}(\lambda,x-\cdot)\right)(y)+e^{-{\rm i}\lambda|x|}v(y)

\displaystyle\times\sum_{j=0}^{l-1}\left((R_{0}^{-}(\lambda^{2m})V)^{j}(R_{0}^{+}(\lambda^{2m})-R_{0}^{-}(\lambda^{2m}))V(R_{0}^{+}(\lambda^{2m})V)^{l-j-1}r^{+}_{2m-n,0}(\lambda,x-\cdot)\right)(y).

Now (
3.11
) follows if we combine (
B.2
), (
B.3
) and (
B.4
), while similar to the proof of (
3.10
), one checks (
3.12
) and (
3.13
) by using (
3.7
) and (
3.8
). Therefore the proof is complete.
